Step 1 — Create infrastructure_engine.py

Run:

touch app/services/infrastructure_engine.py

Then put this in the file:

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.infrastructure import Infrastructure
from app.models.location import Location
from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.location_engine import (
    haversine_distance_km,
)


# Maximum useful distance for each infrastructure category.
CATEGORY_RADII_KM = {
    "airport": 100.0,
    "hospital": 30.0,
    "school": 30.0,
    "shopping": 30.0,
    "road": 20.0,
    "tourism": 50.0,
}


# Maximum distance considered "excellent" for each category.
IDEAL_DISTANCE_KM = {
    "airport": 15.0,
    "hospital": 10.0,
    "school": 10.0,
    "shopping": 10.0,
    "road": 5.0,
    "tourism": 15.0,
}


CATEGORY_WEIGHTS = {
    "airport": 0.15,
    "hospital": 0.20,
    "school": 0.15,
    "shopping": 0.15,
    "road": 0.20,
    "tourism": 0.15,
}


def _distance_score(
    distance_km: float,
    ideal_distance_km: float,
    maximum_distance_km: float,
) -> float:
    """
    Convert distance into a 0-100 proximity score.

    Closer is better.
    """

    if distance_km <= ideal_distance_km:
        return 100.0

    if distance_km >= maximum_distance_km:
        return 0.0

    score = (
        1
        - (
            distance_km - ideal_distance_km
        )
        / (
            maximum_distance_km
            - ideal_distance_km
        )
    ) * 100

    return max(
        0.0,
        min(100.0, score),
    )


def _get_property_location(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> Location | None:
    """
    Resolve the geographic Location associated with a property.
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return None

    if not property_item.location_id:
        return None

    return (
        db.query(Location)
        .filter(
            Location.id == property_item.location_id
        )
        .first()
    )


def _find_nearest(
    db: Session,
    property_location: Location,
    infrastructure_type: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """
    Find the nearest infrastructure item of a specific type.
    """

    if (
        property_location.latitude is None
        or property_location.longitude is None
    ):
        return None

    infrastructure_items = (
        db.query(Infrastructure)
        .filter(
            Infrastructure.infrastructure_type
            == infrastructure_type,
            Infrastructure.latitude.is_not(None),
            Infrastructure.longitude.is_not(None),
        )
        .all()
    )

    nearest = None
    nearest_distance = None

    for item in infrastructure_items:

        distance = haversine_distance_km(
            property_location.latitude,
            property_location.longitude,
            item.latitude,
            item.longitude,
        )

        if (
            nearest_distance is None
            or distance < nearest_distance
        ):
            nearest = item
            nearest_distance = distance

    if nearest is None:
        return None

    return {
        "id": nearest.id,
        "name": nearest.name,
        "type": nearest.infrastructure_type,
        "city": nearest.city,
        "area": nearest.area,
        "distance_km": round(
            nearest_distance,
            2,
        ),
        "importance": nearest.importance,
        "source": nearest.source,
        "source_url": nearest.source_url,
    }


def analyze_property_infrastructure(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Analyze infrastructure surrounding a property.

    Returns:
    - nearest infrastructure by category
    - category scores
    - weighted infrastructure score
    - evidence
    - confidence
    """

    location = _get_property_location(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    if not location:
        return {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "location_found": False,
            "infrastructure_score": None,
            "categories": {},
            "evidence": [],
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }

    if (
        location.latitude is None
        or location.longitude is None
    ):
        return {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "location_found": True,
            "coordinates_available": False,
            "infrastructure_score": None,
            "categories": {},
            "evidence": [],
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }

    categories = {}
    evidence = []

    weighted_score = 0.0
    total_weight = 0.0

    for infrastructure_type, weight in CATEGORY_WEIGHTS.items():

        nearest = _find_nearest(
            db,
            location,
            infrastructure_type,
        )

        if nearest is None:
            continue

        maximum_distance = CATEGORY_RADII_KM[
            infrastructure_type
        ]

        ideal_distance = IDEAL_DISTANCE_KM[
            infrastructure_type
        ]

        score = _distance_score(
            nearest["distance_km"],
            ideal_distance,
            maximum_distance,
        )

        categories[infrastructure_type] = {
            "nearest": nearest,
            "score": round(
                score,
                2,
            ),
            "weight": weight,
        }

        weighted_score += score * weight
        total_weight += weight

        evidence.append(
            {
                "type": infrastructure_type,
                "name": nearest["name"],
                "distance_km": nearest["distance_km"],
                "score": round(score, 2),
                "source": nearest["source"],
                "source_url": nearest["source_url"],
            }
        )

    if total_weight == 0:
        infrastructure_score = None
        confidence = 0.0
    else:
        infrastructure_score = (
            weighted_score / total_weight
        )

        # Confidence increases with category coverage.
        confidence = min(
            1.0,
            total_weight / 1.0,
        )

    return {
        "property_id": property_id,
        "location_found": True,
        "coordinates_available": True,
        "location": {
            "id": location.id,
            "name": location.name,
            "city": location.city,
            "country": location.country,
            "latitude": location.latitude,
            "longitude": location.longitude,
        },
        "categories": categories,
        "infrastructure_score": (
            round(
                infrastructure_score,
                2,
            )
            if infrastructure_score is not None
            else None
        ),
        "evidence": evidence,
        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

One important thing we're doing correctly

The Infrastructure Engine doesn't ask the LLM to calculate these scores.

The flow is:

Coordinates
     ↓
Haversine calculation
     ↓
Distance
     ↓
Deterministic scoring
     ↓
Weighted score
     ↓
Evidence
     ↓
LLM explains it later

That's important for your eventual guardrail architecture.

The LLM should be able to say:

    "AIDA scores well for infrastructure because it has reasonable proximity to the airport, roads and lifestyle amenities."

But the actual distance and score come from our deterministic engine, not Qwen.
After this succeeds

We'll have:

                 INTELLIGENCE CORE

Property
   │
   ▼
Location Engine ──────────► Location Score
   │
   ▼
Infrastructure Engine ───► Infrastructure Score
   │
   ▼
Comparable Engine ───────► Comparable Score
   │
   ▼
Valuation Engine ────────► Valuation Score
   │
   ▼
Growth Engine ───────────► Growth Score
   │
   ▼
Risk Engine ─────────────► Risk Score

Then we combine those into the Deal Engine later.

this was the initial file, please share the updated file
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.services.location_engine import (
    nearby_locations,
)


INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES = {
    "metro",
    "airport",
    "hospital",
    "school",
    "road",
}


def infrastructure_near_property(
    db: Session,
    *,
    latitude: float,
    longitude: float,
    radius_km: float = 10,
) -> dict:

    result = {}

    for location_type in INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES:

        result[location_type] = nearby_locations(
            db,
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            location_type=location_type,
            radius_km=radius_km,
            limit=10,
        )

    return result
def calculate_infrastructure_score(
    infrastructure: dict,
) -> float:

    weights = {
        "metro": 25,
        "airport": 15,
        "hospital": 20,
        "school": 20,
        "road": 20,
    }

    score = 0

    for category, weight in weights.items():

        items = infrastructure.get(
            category,
            [],
        )

        if items:
            nearest = items[0]["distance_km"]

            if nearest <= 1:
                score += weight
            elif nearest <= 3:
                score += weight * 0.7
            elif nearest <= 5:
                score += weight * 0.4

    return round(score, 2)
ChatGPT said:

Yes. Given the infrastructure data is now working, I’d update this service so it also supports shopping and tourism—your current database already contains both—and keeps the scoring weights totaling 100.

Replace the current file with:

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.services.location_engine import (
    nearby_locations,
)


INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES = {
    "metro",
    "airport",
    "hospital",
    "school",
    "road",
    "shopping",
    "tourism",
}


def infrastructure_near_property(
    db: Session,
    *,
    latitude: float,
    longitude: float,
    radius_km: float = 10,
) -> dict:
    """
    Find nearby infrastructure around a property.

    Returns infrastructure grouped by type.
    """

    result = {}

    for location_type in INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES:
        result[location_type] = nearby_locations(
            db,
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            location_type=location_type,
            radius_km=radius_km,
            limit=10,
        )

    return result


def calculate_infrastructure_score(
    infrastructure: dict,
) -> float:
    """
    Calculate an infrastructure score from 0-100.

    The score is based on the nearest infrastructure item
    in each category.
    """

    weights = {
        "metro": 15,
        "airport": 10,
        "hospital": 15,
        "school": 15,
        "road": 15,
        "shopping": 15,
        "tourism": 15,
    }

    score = 0.0

    for category, weight in weights.items():
        items = infrastructure.get(
            category,
            [],
        )

        if not items:
            continue

        nearest = items[0].get("distance_km")

        if nearest is None:
            continue

        if nearest <= 1:
            score += weight
        elif nearest <= 3:
            score += weight * 0.7
        elif nearest <= 5:
            score += weight * 0.4

    return round(score, 2)

Why this update

Your infrastructure table currently has:

    airport
    hospital
    school
    shopping
    road
    tourism

So the old file would completely ignore shopping and tourism.

The updated weights are:

metro       15
airport     10
hospital    15
school      15
road        15
shopping    15
tourism     15
----------------
TOTAL      100

The existing distance logic stays intact:

0–1 km    = 100% of category weight
1–3 km    = 70%
3–5 km    = 40%
>5 km     = 0

One important point: don't add coordinates or database queries to this file. Your nearby_locations() function in location_engine.py should remain responsible for geographic lookup. This service should remain the scoring/orchestration layer.

You said:

where to put this data
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.infrastructure import Infrastructure
from app.models.location import Location
from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.location_engine import (
    haversine_distance_km,
)


# Maximum useful distance for each infrastructure category.
CATEGORY_RADII_KM = {
    "airport": 100.0,
    "hospital": 30.0,
    "school": 30.0,
    "shopping": 30.0,
    "road": 20.0,
    "tourism": 50.0,
}


# Maximum distance considered "excellent" for each category.
IDEAL_DISTANCE_KM = {
    "airport": 15.0,
    "hospital": 10.0,
    "school": 10.0,
    "shopping": 10.0,
    "road": 5.0,
    "tourism": 15.0,
}


CATEGORY_WEIGHTS = {
    "airport": 0.15,
    "hospital": 0.20,
    "school": 0.15,
    "shopping": 0.15,
    "road": 0.20,
    "tourism": 0.15,
}


def _distance_score(
    distance_km: float,
    ideal_distance_km: float,
    maximum_distance_km: float,
) -> float:
    """
    Convert distance into a 0-100 proximity score.

    Closer is better.
    """

    if distance_km <= ideal_distance_km:
        return 100.0

    if distance_km >= maximum_distance_km:
        return 0.0

    score = (
        1
        - (
            distance_km - ideal_distance_km
        )
        / (
            maximum_distance_km
            - ideal_distance_km
        )
    ) * 100

    return max(
        0.0,
        min(100.0, score),
    )


def _get_property_location(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> Location | None:
    """
    Resolve the geographic Location associated with a property.
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return None

    if not property_item.location_id:
        return None

    return (
        db.query(Location)
        .filter(
            Location.id == property_item.location_id
        )
        .first()
    )


def _find_nearest(
    db: Session,
    property_location: Location,
    infrastructure_type: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """
    Find the nearest infrastructure item of a specific type.
    """

    if (
        property_location.latitude is None
        or property_location.longitude is None
    ):
        return None

    infrastructure_items = (
        db.query(Infrastructure)
        .filter(
            Infrastructure.infrastructure_type
            == infrastructure_type,
            Infrastructure.latitude.is_not(None),
            Infrastructure.longitude.is_not(None),
        )
        .all()
    )

    nearest = None
    nearest_distance = None

    for item in infrastructure_items:

        distance = haversine_distance_km(
            property_location.latitude,
            property_location.longitude,
            item.latitude,
            item.longitude,
        )

        if (
            nearest_distance is None
            or distance < nearest_distance
        ):
            nearest = item
            nearest_distance = distance

    if nearest is None:
        return None

    return {
        "id": nearest.id,
        "name": nearest.name,
        "type": nearest.infrastructure_type,
        "city": nearest.city,
        "area": nearest.area,
        "distance_km": round(
            nearest_distance,
            2,
        ),
        "importance": nearest.importance,
        "source": nearest.source,
        "source_url": nearest.source_url,
    }


def analyze_property_infrastructure(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Analyze infrastructure surrounding a property.

    Returns:
    - nearest infrastructure by category
    - category scores
    - weighted infrastructure score
    - evidence
    - confidence
    """

    location = _get_property_location(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    if not location:
        return {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "location_found": False,
            "infrastructure_score": None,
            "categories": {},
            "evidence": [],
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }

    if (
        location.latitude is None
        or location.longitude is None
    ):
        return {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "location_found": True,
            "coordinates_available": False,
            "infrastructure_score": None,
            "categories": {},
            "evidence": [],
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }

    categories = {}
    evidence = []

    weighted_score = 0.0
    total_weight = 0.0

    for infrastructure_type, weight in CATEGORY_WEIGHTS.items():

        nearest = _find_nearest(
            db,
            location,
            infrastructure_type,
        )

        if nearest is None:
            continue

        maximum_distance = CATEGORY_RADII_KM[
            infrastructure_type
        ]

        ideal_distance = IDEAL_DISTANCE_KM[
            infrastructure_type
        ]

        score = _distance_score(
            nearest["distance_km"],
            ideal_distance,
            maximum_distance,
        )

        categories[infrastructure_type] = {
            "nearest": nearest,
            "score": round(
                score,
                2,
            ),
            "weight": weight,
        }

        weighted_score += score * weight
        total_weight += weight

        evidence.append(
            {
                "type": infrastructure_type,
                "name": nearest["name"],
                "distance_km": nearest["distance_km"],
                "score": round(score, 2),
                "source": nearest["source"],
                "source_url": nearest["source_url"],
            }
        )

    if total_weight == 0:
        infrastructure_score = None
        confidence = 0.0
    else:
        infrastructure_score = (
            weighted_score / total_weight
        )

        # Confidence increases with category coverage.
        confidence = min(
            1.0,
            total_weight / 1.0,
        )

    return {
        "property_id": property_id,
        "location_found": True,
        "coordinates_available": True,
        "location": {
            "id": location.id,
            "name": location.name,
            "city": location.city,
            "country": location.country,
            "latitude": location.latitude,
            "longitude": location.longitude,
        },
        "categories": categories,
        "infrastructure_score": (
            round(
                infrastructure_score,
                2,
            )
            if infrastructure_score is not None
            else None
        ),
        "evidence": evidence,
        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

                "distance_km": nearest["distance_km"],
                "score": round(score, 2),
                "source": nearest["source"],
                "source_url": nearest["source_url"],
            }
        )

    if total_weight == 0:
        infrastructure_score = None
        confidence = 0.0
    else:
        infrastructure_score = (
            weighted_score / total_weight
        )

        # Confidence increases with category coverage.
        confidence = min(
            1.0,
            total_weight / 1.0,
        )

    return {
        "property_id": property_id,
        "location_found": True,
        "coordinates_available": True,
        "location": {
            "id": location.id,
            "name": location.name,
            "city": location.city,
            "country": location.country,
            "latitude": location.latitude,
            "longitude": location.longitude,
        },
        "categories": categories,
        "infrastructure_score": (
            round(
                infrastructure_score,
                2,
            )
            if infrastructure_score is not None
            else None
        ),
        "evidence": evidence,
        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

where to add this code in this file

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.services.location_engine import (
    nearby_locations,
)


INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES = {
    "metro",
    "airport",
    "hospital",
    "school",
    "road",
    "shopping",
    "tourism",
}


def infrastructure_near_property(
    db: Session,
    *,
    latitude: float,
    longitude: float,
    radius_km: float = 10,
) -> dict:
    """
    Find nearby infrastructure around a property.

    Returns infrastructure grouped by type.
    """

    result = {}

    for location_type in INFRASTRUCTURE_TYPES:
        result[location_type] = nearby_locations(
            db,
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            location_type=location_type,
            radius_km=radius_km,
            limit=10,
        )

    return result


def calculate_infrastructure_score(
    infrastructure: dict,
) -> float:
    """
    Calculate an infrastructure score from 0-100.

    The score is based on the nearest infrastructure item
    in each category.
    """

    weights = {
        "metro": 15,
        "airport": 10,
        "hospital": 15,
        "school": 15,
        "road": 15,
        "shopping": 15,
        "tourism": 15,
    }

    score = 0.0

    for category, weight in weights.items():
        items = infrastructure.get(
            category,
            [],
        )

        if not items:
            continue

        nearest = items[0].get("distance_km")

        if nearest is None:
            continue

        if nearest <= 1:
            score += weight
        elif nearest <= 3:
            score += weight * 0.7
        elif nearest <= 5:
            score += weight * 0.4

    return round(score, 2)

Step 4 — Comparable Engine

The purpose is:

    Given a property, find other properties that are reasonably comparable and calculate a comparable-market benchmark.

For your current POC, we don't need a new database table yet. We can use your existing properties table.

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.property import Property


def _similarity_score(
    target: Property,
    comparable: Property,
) -> float:
    """
    Calculate a simple 0-100 similarity score.

    Higher means the comparable is more similar to the
    target property.
    """

    score = 0.0
    possible = 0.0

    # Property type
    if target.property_type and comparable.property_type:
        possible += 25

        if (
            target.property_type.strip().lower()
            == comparable.property_type.strip().lower()
        ):
            score += 25

    # City
    if target.city and comparable.city:
        possible += 20

        if (
            target.city.strip().lower()
            == comparable.city.strip().lower()
        ):
            score += 20

    # Bedrooms
    if (
        target.bedrooms is not None
        and comparable.bedrooms is not None
    ):
        possible += 20

        difference = abs(
            target.bedrooms
            - comparable.bedrooms
        )

        if difference == 0:
            score += 20
        elif difference == 1:
            score += 12

    # Area
    if (
        target.area is not None
        and comparable.area is not None
        and target.area > 0
    ):
        possible += 20

        area_difference = abs(
            comparable.area - target.area
        ) / target.area

        if area_difference <= 0.10:
            score += 20
        elif area_difference <= 0.25:
            score += 12
        elif area_difference <= 0.40:
            score += 6

    # Developer
    if target.developer and comparable.developer:
        possible += 15

        if (
            target.developer.strip().lower()
            == comparable.developer.strip().lower()
        ):
            score += 15

    if possible == 0:
        return 0.0

    return round(
        (score / possible) * 100,
        2,
    )


def find_comparables(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
    limit: int = 10,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Find properties comparable to the target property.
    """

    target = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not target:
        return {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "property_found": False,
            "comparables": [],
            "comparable_count": 0,
        }

    query = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id != property_id)
    )

    # Prefer properties in the same city.
    if target.city:
        query = query.filter(
            Property.city == target.city
        )

    # Prefer the same property type.
    if target.property_type:
        query = query.filter(
            Property.property_type
            == target.property_type
        )

    candidates = query.all()

    scored = []

    for comparable in candidates:
        similarity = _similarity_score(
            target,
            comparable,
        )

        if similarity <= 0:
            continue

        scored.append(
            {
                "property": comparable,
                "similarity_score": similarity,
            }
        )

    scored.sort(
        key=lambda item: item["similarity_score"],
        reverse=True,
    )

    selected = scored[:limit]

    results = []

    for item in selected:
        comparable = item["property"]

        results.append(
            {
                "id": comparable.id,
                "title": comparable.title,
                "project_name": comparable.project_name,
                "developer": comparable.developer,
                "property_type": comparable.property_type,
                "city": comparable.city,
                "location": comparable.location,
                "price": comparable.price,
                "currency": comparable.currency,
                "bedrooms": comparable.bedrooms,
                "bathrooms": comparable.bathrooms,
                "area": comparable.area,
                "area_unit": comparable.area_unit,
                "similarity_score": item[
                    "similarity_score"
                ],
                "source": comparable.source,
                "source_url": comparable.source_url,
            }
        )

    return {
        "property_id": property_id,
        "property_found": True,
        "target": {
            "id": target.id,
            "title": target.title,
            "project_name": target.project_name,
            "developer": target.developer,
            "property_type": target.property_type,
            "city": target.city,
            "price": target.price,
            "currency": target.currency,
            "bedrooms": target.bedrooms,
            "area": target.area,
            "area_unit": target.area_unit,
        },
        "comparables": results,
        "comparable_count": len(results),
    }


def calculate_comparable_metrics(
    comparable_result: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Calculate simple market metrics from comparable properties.
    """

    comparables = comparable_result.get(
        "comparables",
        [],
    )

    priced = [
        item
        for item in comparables
        if item.get("price") is not None
        and item.get("price") > 0
    ]

    if not priced:
        return {
            "comparable_count": len(comparables),
            "priced_comparables": 0,
            "average_price": None,
            "median_price": None,
            "average_price_per_area": None,
        }

    prices = [
        item["price"]
        for item in priced
    ]

    prices_sorted = sorted(prices)

    average_price = (
        sum(prices)
        / len(prices)
    )

    middle = len(prices_sorted) // 2

    if len(prices_sorted) % 2 == 0:
        median_price = (
            prices_sorted[middle - 1]
            + prices_sorted[middle]
        ) / 2
    else:
        median_price = prices_sorted[middle]

    price_per_area = []

    for item in priced:
        area = item.get("area")

        if area and area > 0:
            price_per_area.append(
                item["price"] / area
            )

    average_price_per_area = None

    if price_per_area:
        average_price_per_area = (
            sum(price_per_area)
            / len(price_per_area)
        )

    return {
        "comparable_count": len(comparables),
        "priced_comparables": len(priced),
        "average_price": round(
            average_price,
            2,
        ),
        "median_price": round(
            median_price,
            2,
        ),
        "average_price_per_area": (
            round(
                average_price_per_area,
                2,
            )
            if average_price_per_area is not None
            else None
        ),
    }

def calculate_comparable_metrics(
    comparable_result: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Calculate simple market metrics from comparable properties.
    """

    comparables = comparable_result.get(
        "comparables",
        [],

        item
        for item in comparables
        if item.get("price") is not None
        and item.get("price") > 0

    prices = [
        item["price"]
        for item in priced
    ]

    prices_sorted = sorted(prices)

    average_price = (
        sum(prices)
        / len(prices)
    )

    middle = len(prices_sorted) // 2

    if len(prices_sorted) % 2 == 0:
        median_price = (
            prices_sorted[middle - 1]
            + prices_sorted[middle]
        ) / 2
    else:
        median_price = prices_sorted[middle]

    price_per_area = []

    for item in priced:
        area = item.get("area")

        if area and area > 0:
            price_per_area.append(
                item["price"] / area
            )

    average_price_per_area = None

    if price_per_area:
        average_price_per_area = (
            sum(price_per_area)
            / len(price_per_area)
        )

    return {
        "comparable_count": len(comparables),
        "priced_comparables": len(priced),
        "average_price": round(
            average_price,
            2,
        ),
        "median_price": round(
            median_price,
            2,
        ),
        "average_price_per_area": (
            round(
                average_price_per_area,
                2,
            )
            if average_price_per_area is not None
            else None
        ),
    }

Your current find_comparables() requires:

    Same city, when the target has a city.
    Same property_type, when the target has a property type.
    At least one matching attribute to produce a similarity score.

For property 10, that means a comparable must be:

city = Muscat
property_type = hotel_residences

Your Maldives property cannot qualify.

Excellent. The Comparable Engine is now verified. Let's move to the next step: build the Property Analysis service that combines location, infrastructure, and comparable data.
Step 1 — Create property_analysis.py

Create:

nano app/services/property_analysis.py

Put this entire file in it:

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.comparable_engine import (
    calculate_comparable_metrics,
    find_comparables,
)
from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
    analyze_property_infrastructure,
)


def analyze_property(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Generate a combined intelligence report for a property.

    Combines:
    - property information
    - location information
    - infrastructure analysis
    - comparable properties
    - comparable market metrics
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return {
            "property_found": False,
            "property_id": property_id,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Location
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    location = None

    if property_item.location_record:
        location = {
            "id": property_item.location_record.id,
            "name": property_item.location_record.name,
            "type": property_item.location_record.location_type,
            "city": property_item.location_record.city,
            "country": property_item.location_record.country,
            "area": property_item.location_record.area,
            "address": property_item.location_record.address,
            "latitude": property_item.location_record.latitude,
            "longitude": property_item.location_record.longitude,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Infrastructure
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Comparable properties
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    comparable_result = find_comparables(
        db,
        property_id=property_id,
        limit=10,
    )

    comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
        comparable_result,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Overall confidence
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure_confidence = infrastructure.get(
        "confidence",
        0.0,
    )

    comparable_count = comparable_result.get(
        "comparable_count",
        0,
    )

    if comparable_count >= 5:
        comparable_confidence = 1.0
    elif comparable_count >= 3:
        comparable_confidence = 0.8
    elif comparable_count >= 1:
        comparable_confidence = 0.5
    else:
        comparable_confidence = 0.0

    confidence = (
        infrastructure_confidence * 0.5
        + comparable_confidence * 0.5
    )

    return {
        "property_found": True,

        "property": {
            "id": property_item.id,
            "title": property_item.title,
            "project_name": property_item.project_name,
            "developer": property_item.developer,
            "source": property_item.source,
            "property_type": property_item.property_type,
            "listing_type": property_item.listing_type,
            "country": property_item.country,
            "city": property_item.city,
            "location": property_item.location,
            "price": property_item.price,
            "currency": property_item.currency,
            "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
            "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
            "area": property_item.area,
            "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
            "status": property_item.status,
            "completion_date": property_item.completion_date,
            "brands": property_item.brands,
            "source_url": property_item.source_url,
        },

        "location": location,

        "infrastructure": infrastructure,

        "comparables": {
            "items": comparable_result.get(
                "comparables",
                [],
            ),
            "count": comparable_result.get(
                "comparable_count",
                0,
            ),
            "metrics": comparable_metrics,
        },

        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

Save it.

Great. Your API structure is clear now. The next step should be exposing the infrastructure + comparable analysis through API endpoints, rather than changing the engines again.

Given your files, I recommend adding a new API module:

app/api/analysis.py

This keeps comparable_engine.py and infrastructure_engine.py as business-logic services.
1. Create app/api/analysis.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db
from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.comparable_engine import (
    calculate_comparable_metrics,
    find_comparables,
)
from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
    analyze_property_infrastructure,
)


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/properties",
    tags=["property-analysis"],
)


@router.get("/{property_id}/infrastructure")
def property_infrastructure(
    property_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Analyze infrastructure surrounding a property.
    """

    result = analyze_property_infrastructure(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    if not result.get("location_found"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Property or property location not found",
        )

    return result


@router.get("/{property_id}/comparables")
def property_comparables(
    property_id: int,
    limit: int = 10,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Find comparable properties and calculate market metrics.
    """

    if limit < 1 or limit > 50:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="limit must be between 1 and 50",
        )

    result = find_comparables(
        db,
        property_id=property_id,
        limit=limit,
    )

    if not result.get("property_found"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Property not found",
        )

    metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
        result,
    )

    return {
        **result,
        "metrics": metrics,
    }


@router.get("/{property_id}/analysis")
def property_analysis(
    property_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Return combined infrastructure and comparable analysis.
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Property not found",
        )

    infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    comparables = find_comparables(
        db,
        property_id=property_id,
        limit=10,
    )

    comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
        comparables,
    )

    return {
        "property": {
            "id": property_item.id,
            "title": property_item.title,
            "project_name": property_item.project_name,
            "developer": property_item.developer,
            "property_type": property_item.property_type,
            "city": property_item.city,
            "country": property_item.country,
            "price": property_item.price,
            "currency": property_item.currency,
            "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
            "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
            "area": property_item.area,
            "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
        },
        "infrastructure": infrastructure,
        "comparables": comparables,
        "comparable_metrics": comparable_metrics,
    }

ChatGPT said:

Excellent — all three analysis endpoints are working correctly. You now have the core property intelligence layer working end-to-end.

The next logical step is to connect this intelligence to your existing /api/chat endpoint.
Next step: upgrade /api/chat

Right now, a query such as:

Show me properties in Oman

returns property records.

We want the chat layer to eventually understand requests such as:

Show me properties in Oman

and return:

    matching properties
    location information
    infrastructure score
    nearby airport/hospital/school/shopping/road/tourism
    comparable properties
    comparable market metrics
    evidence/source information

For example, property 10 already has:

Infrastructure score: 71.55

and:

Average comparable price: $966,666.67
Median comparable price:  $950,000
Average price/sqm:        $4,518.81

So the intelligence itself is working. We should not modify those engines now.
Before changing chat.py

Please run:

sed -n '1,260p' app/api/chat.py

Send me the output.

I'll then show you the exact changes to app/api/chat.py, keeping your existing property-search behavior intact and adding the intelligence layer on top.

The flow will become:

User
  │
  ▼
/api/chat
  │
  ├── Query understanding
  │
  ├── Property search
  │
  └── Property intelligence
        │
        ├── Location
        ├── Infrastructure
        ├── Comparables
        └── Market metrics

This is the right point to integrate rather than adding more standalone endpoints.
You said:

sed -n '1,260p' app/api/chat.py
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db

from app.schemas.property import (
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
)

from app.services.query_understanding import (
    understand_query,
)

from app.services.hybrid_search import (
    hybrid_search,
)


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/chat",
    tags=["chat"],
)


def build_message(
    query: str,
    understood: dict,
    results: list[dict],
    semantic_results: list[dict],
) -> str:

    if not results and not semantic_results:
        return (
            "I couldn't find any information matching "
            f'your query: "{query}".'
        )

    if not results and semantic_results:
        return (
            "I found relevant information for your query."
        )


    if len(results) == 1:

        property_item = results[0]

        name = (
            property_item.get("project_name")
            or property_item.get("title")
            or "This property"
        )

        location_parts = [
            property_item.get("location"),
            property_item.get("city"),
            property_item.get("country"),
        ]

        location = ", ".join(
            str(part)
            for part in location_parts
            if part
        )

        developer = property_item.get(
            "developer"
        )

        brands = property_item.get(
            "brands"
        )

        brand = None

        if brands:
            brand = ", ".join(
                str(item)
                for item in brands
            )

        parts = [
            f"{name} is available"
        ]

        if location:
            parts.append(
                f"at {location}"
            )

        if developer:
            parts.append(
                f"and is developed by {developer}"
            )

        if brand:
            parts.append(
                f"with {brand} branding"
            )

        return " ".join(parts) + "."

    return (
        f"I found {len(results)} properties "
        "matching your query."
    )

@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=ChatResponse,
)
def chat(
    request: ChatRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = request.message.strip()

    understood = understand_query(query)

    retrieval = hybrid_search(
        db=db,
        query=query,
        filters=understood,
        brand=understood.get("brand"),
        country=understood.get("country"),
        city=understood.get("city"),
        property_type=understood.get("property_type"),
    )

    structured_results = retrieval["results"]
    semantic_results = retrieval["semantic_results"]

    message = build_message(
        query=query,
        understood=understood,
        results=structured_results,
        semantic_results=semantic_results,
    )

    return {
        "message": message,
        "intent": understood["intent"],
        "filters": {
            key: value
            for key, value in understood.items()
            if key != "query"
            and value is not None
        },
        "results": structured_results,
        "semantic_results": semantic_results,
    }
ChatGPT said:

Yes. Your chat.py is a good clean starting point. Do not replace the search logic. We will add intelligence after structured_results are obtained.

The safest next change is to make /api/chat return an analysis section for each matching property.
Step 1 — Update app/api/chat.py

Replace the current file with this version:

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db

from app.schemas.property import (
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
)

from app.services.query_understanding import (
    understand_query,
)

from app.services.hybrid_search import (
    hybrid_search,
)

from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
    analyze_property_infrastructure,
)

from app.services.comparable_engine import (
    find_comparables,
    calculate_comparable_metrics,
)


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/chat",
    tags=["chat"],
)


def build_message(
    query: str,
    understood: dict,
    results: list[dict],
    semantic_results: list[dict],
) -> str:

    if not results and not semantic_results:
        return (
            "I couldn't find any information matching "
            f'your query: "{query}".'
        )

    if not results and semantic_results:
        return (
            "I found relevant information for your query."
        )

    if len(results) == 1:

        property_item = results[0]

        name = (
            property_item.get("project_name")
            or property_item.get("title")
            or "This property"
        )

        location_parts = [
            property_item.get("location"),
            property_item.get("city"),
            property_item.get("country"),
        ]

        location = ", ".join(
            str(part)
            for part in location_parts
            if part
        )

        developer = property_item.get(
            "developer"
        )

        brands = property_item.get(
            "brands"
        )

        brand = None

        if brands:
            brand = ", ".join(
                str(item)
                for item in brands
            )

        parts = [
            f"{name} is available"
        ]

        if location:
            parts.append(
                f"at {location}"
            )

        if developer:
            parts.append(
                f"and is developed by {developer}"
            )

        if brand:
            parts.append(
                f"with {brand} branding"
            )

        return " ".join(parts) + "."

    return (
        f"I found {len(results)} properties "
        "matching your query."
    )


def build_property_analysis(
    db: Session,
    results: list[dict],
) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Build intelligence analysis for each property.

    Includes:
    - infrastructure analysis
    - comparable properties
    - comparable market metrics
    """

    analysis = []

    for property_item in results:

        property_id = property_item.get("id")

        if property_id is None:
            continue

        infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
            db,
            property_id,
        )

        comparables = find_comparables(
            db,
            property_id=property_id,
            limit=10,
        )

        comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
            comparables,
        )

        analysis.append(
            {
                "property_id": property_id,
                "infrastructure": infrastructure,
                "comparables": comparables,
                "comparable_metrics": comparable_metrics,
            }
        )

    return analysis


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=ChatResponse,
)
def chat(
    request: ChatRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = request.message.strip()

    understood = understand_query(query)

    retrieval = hybrid_search(
        db=db,
        query=query,
        filters=understood,
        brand=understood.get("brand"),
        country=understood.get("country"),
        city=understood.get("city"),
        property_type=understood.get("property_type"),
    )

    structured_results = retrieval["results"]

    semantic_results = retrieval["semantic_results"]

    message = build_message(
        query=query,
        understood=understood,
        results=structured_results,
        semantic_results=semantic_results,
    )

    analysis = build_property_analysis(
        db,
        structured_results,
    )

    return {
        "message": message,
        "intent": understood["intent"],
        "filters": {
            key: value
            for key, value in understood.items()
            if key != "query"
            and value is not None
        },
        "results": structured_results,
        "semantic_results": semantic_results,
        "analysis": analysis,
    }

Important: there may be one schema issue

Your endpoint currently has:

response_model=ChatResponse

If ChatResponse does not contain an analysis field, FastAPI/Pydantic may omit it or reject the response depending on your schema configuration.

So don't test yet.

First run:

sed -n '1,260p' app/schemas/property.py

Send me that output.

I'll update ChatResponse next so the new:

"analysis": [...]

is properly supported.
What we're deliberately doing

We're keeping your existing flow:

User query
    ↓
understand_query()
    ↓
hybrid_search()
    ↓
structured results
    ↓
build_property_analysis()
    ├── infrastructure_engine
    └── comparable_engine
    ↓
ChatResponse

This means your existing property-search behavior remains intact while the new intelligence is added alongside it.

After the schema is updated, a query such as:

curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"message":"Show me Marriott Residences Oman"}'

will be able to return both the property and its intelligence.

sed -n '1,260p' app/schemas/property.py
from datetime import datetime

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class PropertyBase(BaseModel):
    source: str
    external_id: str | None = None

    title: str
    project_name: str | None = None
    developer: str | None = None

    listing_type: str | None = None
    property_type: str | None = None

    country: str | None = None
    city: str | None = None
    location: str | None = None

    price: float | None = None
    currency: str | None = None

    bedrooms: int | None = None
    bathrooms: int | None = None

    area: float | None = None
    area_unit: str | None = None

    status: str | None = None
    completion_date: str | None = None

    description: str | None = None

    amenities: list[str] | None = None
    brands: list[str] | None = None
    images: list[str] | None = None

    source_url: str
    raw_content: str | None = None

    latitude: float | None = None
    longitude: float | None = None



class PropertyResponse(PropertyBase):
    id: int
    scraped_at: datetime

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str


class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict](.venv)
ChatGPT said:

Yes — this confirms the only schema change we need right now.

Your ChatResponse currently ends with:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]

We need to add:

analysis: list[dict]

Step 1 — Update app/schemas/property.py

You can simply change the bottom section to:

class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str


class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]
    analysis: list[dict]

That's the only schema change required.

You don't need to change PropertyBase or PropertyResponse.
Step 2 — Restart the API

If you're running:

uvicorn app.main:app --reload

the reload should happen automatically.

Otherwise restart your FastAPI server.

One thing to watch for

Your current chat.py calls:

build_property_analysis(
    db,
    structured_results,
)

So analysis is generated only for structured_results, not semantic-only results. That's intentional for this first integration because infrastructure/comparables require an actual database property_id.

Run the test above and send me the JSON response. If that works, the next step should be making the chat response itself explain the intelligence in natural language, e.g.:

    Marriott Residences Oman is in AIDA, Muscat. Its infrastructure score is 71.55/100, with the nearest airport 12.8 km away. Three comparable properties were found, with a median asking price of $950,000.

That will turn the backend from a search API into an actual real-estate intelligence API.

sed -n '1,320p' app/services/property_analysis.py
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.comparable_engine import (
    calculate_comparable_metrics,
    find_comparables,
)
from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
    analyze_property_infrastructure,
)


def analyze_property(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Generate a combined intelligence report for a property.

    Combines:
    - property information
    - location information
    - infrastructure analysis
    - comparable properties
    - comparable market metrics
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return {
            "property_found": False,
            "property_id": property_id,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Location
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    location = None

    if property_item.location_record:
        location = {
            "id": property_item.location_record.id,
            "name": property_item.location_record.name,
            "type": property_item.location_record.location_type,
            "city": property_item.location_record.city,
            "country": property_item.location_record.country,
            "area": property_item.location_record.area,
            "address": property_item.location_record.address,
            "latitude": property_item.location_record.latitude,
            "longitude": property_item.location_record.longitude,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Infrastructure
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Comparable properties
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    comparable_result = find_comparables(
        db,
        property_id=property_id,
        limit=10,
    )

    comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
        comparable_result,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Overall confidence
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure_confidence = infrastructure.get(
        "confidence",
        0.0,
    )

    comparable_count = comparable_result.get(
        "comparable_count",
        0,
    )

    if comparable_count >= 5:
        comparable_confidence = 1.0
    elif comparable_count >= 3:
        comparable_confidence = 0.8
    elif comparable_count >= 1:
        comparable_confidence = 0.5
    else:
        comparable_confidence = 0.0

    confidence = (
        infrastructure_confidence * 0.5
        + comparable_confidence * 0.5
    )

    return {
        "property_found": True,

        "property": {
            "id": property_item.id,
            "title": property_item.title,
            "project_name": property_item.project_name,
            "developer": property_item.developer,
            "source": property_item.source,
            "property_type": property_item.property_type,
            "listing_type": property_item.listing_type,
            "country": property_item.country,
            "city": property_item.city,
            "location": property_item.location,
            "price": property_item.price,
            "currency": property_item.currency,
            "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
            "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
            "area": property_item.area,
            "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
            "status": property_item.status,
            "completion_date": property_item.completion_date,
            "brands": property_item.brands,
            "source_url": property_item.source_url,
        },

        "location": location,

        "infrastructure": infrastructure,

        "comparables": {
            "items": comparable_result.get(
                "comparables",
                [],
            ),
            "count": comparable_result.get(
                "comparable_count",
                0,
            ),
            "metrics": comparable_metrics,
        },

        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

Updated app/api/chat.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db

from app.schemas.property import (
ChatRequest,
ChatResponse,
)

from app.services.query_understanding import (
understand_query,
)

from app.services.hybrid_search import (
hybrid_search,
)

from app.services.property_analysis import (
analyze_property,
)

router = APIRouter(
prefix="/api/chat",
tags=["chat"],
)

def build_message(
query: str,
understood: dict,
results: list[dict],
semantic_results: list[dict],
analyses: list[dict],
) -> str:
"""
Build a concise response using search and property intelligence.
"""

if not results and not semantic_results:
    return (
        "I couldn't find any information matching "
        f'your query: "{query}".'
    )

if not results and semantic_results:
    return (
        "I found relevant information for your query."
    )

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Single property
# ---------------------------------------------------------

if len(results) == 1:

    property_item = results[0]

    name = (
        property_item.get("project_name")
        or property_item.get("title")
        or "This property"
    )

    location_parts = [
        property_item.get("location"),
        property_item.get("city"),
        property_item.get("country"),
    ]

    location = ", ".join(
        str(part)
        for part in location_parts
        if part
    )

    developer = property_item.get(
        "developer"
    )

    brands = property_item.get(
        "brands"
    )

    brand = None

    if brands:
        brand = ", ".join(
            str(item)
            for item in brands
        )

    parts = [
        f"{name} is available"
    ]

    if location:
        parts.append(
            f"at {location}"
        )

    if developer:
        parts.append(
            f"and is developed by {developer}"
        )

    if brand:
        parts.append(
            f"with {brand} branding"
        )

    message = " ".join(parts) + "."

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # Add intelligence summary
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    if analyses:

        analysis = analyses[0]

        infrastructure = analysis.get(
            "infrastructure",
            {},
        )

        infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
            "infrastructure_score"
        )

        if infrastructure_score is not None:
            message += (
                f" The infrastructure score is "
                f"{infrastructure_score}/100."
            )

        comparables = analysis.get(
            "comparables",
            {},
        )

        metrics = comparables.get(
            "metrics",
            {},
        )

        comparable_count = comparables.get(
            "count",
            0,
        )

        if comparable_count:
            message += (
                f" I found {comparable_count} "
                "comparable properties."
            )

        median_price = metrics.get(
            "median_price"
        )

        if median_price is not None:
            message += (
                f" The comparable median price is "
                f"${median_price:,.2f}."
            )

    return message

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Multiple properties
# ---------------------------------------------------------

return (
    f"I found {len(results)} properties "
    "matching your query."
)

@router.post(
"",
response_model=ChatResponse,
)
def chat(
request: ChatRequest,
db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
query = request.message.strip()

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Understand query
# ---------------------------------------------------------

understood = understand_query(
    query
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Hybrid property search
# ---------------------------------------------------------

retrieval = hybrid_search(
    db=db,
    query=query,
    filters=understood,
    brand=understood.get("brand"),
    country=understood.get("country"),
    city=understood.get("city"),
    property_type=understood.get("property_type"),
)

structured_results = retrieval[
    "results"
]

semantic_results = retrieval[
    "semantic_results"
]

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Property intelligence
# ---------------------------------------------------------

analyses = []

for property_item in structured_results:

    property_id = property_item.get(
        "id"
    )

    if property_id is None:
        continue

    analysis = analyze_property(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    if analysis.get(
        "property_found"
    ):
        analyses.append(
            analysis
        )

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Build response
# ---------------------------------------------------------

message = build_message(
    query=query,
    understood=understood,
    results=structured_results,
    semantic_results=semantic_results,
    analyses=analyses,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# API response
# ---------------------------------------------------------

return {
    "message": message,
    "intent": understood["intent"],
    "filters": {
        key: value
        for key, value in understood.items()
        if key != "query"
        and value is not None
    },
    "results": structured_results,
    "semantic_results": semantic_results,
    "analysis": analyses,
}

Step 2 — Update ChatResponse

There is one important issue.

Your current schema ends with:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]

But the new API returns:

"analysis": analyses

So add analysis to the schema.

Change:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]

to:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]
    analysis: list[dict]

analysis = []

for property_item in structured_results:

    if property_id is None:
        continue

    property_analysis = analyze_property(
        db,

return {
    "message": message,
    "intent": understood["intent"],
    "filters": {
        key: value
        for key, value in understood.items()
        if key != "query"
        and value is not None
    },
    "results": structured_results,
    "semantic_results": semantic_results,
    "analysis": analysis,
}

2. Update ChatResponse

Your current schema ends with:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]

Change it to:

class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str
    intent: str
    filters: dict
    results: list[dict]
    semantic_results: list[dict]
    analysis: list[dict] = []

This gives the API a proper place for the intelligence output.

Step 3 — Upgrade build_message()

Right now this:

Marriott Residences Oman is available at AIDA, Muscat, Oman and is developed by DarGlobal with Marriott branding.

doesn't use the analysis we just calculated.

We want the response to use the infrastructure and comparable data.
Replace build_message() in app/api/chat.py

Use this version:
Updated build_message function

def build_message(
    query: str,
    understood: dict,
    results: list[dict],
    semantic_results: list[dict],
    analysis: list[dict] | None = None,
) -> str:

    if not results and not semantic_results:
        return (
            "I couldn't find any information matching "
            f'your query: "{query}".'
        )

    if not results and semantic_results:
        return (
            "I found relevant information for your query."
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Basic property response
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    if len(results) == 1:

        property_item = results[0]

        name = (
            property_item.get("project_name")
            or property_item.get("title")
            or "This property"
        )

        location_parts = [
            property_item.get("location"),
            property_item.get("city"),
            property_item.get("country"),
        ]

        location = ", ".join(
            str(part)
            for part in location_parts
            if part
        )

        developer = property_item.get(
            "developer"
        )

        brands = property_item.get(
            "brands"
        )

        brand = None

        if brands:
            brand = ", ".join(
                str(item)
                for item in brands
            )

        message = (
            f"{name} is available"
        )

        if location:
            message += f" at {location}"

        if developer:
            message += (
                f" and is developed by {developer}"
            )

        if brand:
            message += (
                f" with {brand} branding"
            )

        message += "."

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Intelligence analysis
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        if analysis:

            property_analysis = analysis[0].get(
                "analysis",
                {}
            )

            infrastructure = property_analysis.get(
                "infrastructure",
                {}
            )

            infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
                "infrastructure_score"
            )

            if infrastructure_score is not None:
                message += (
                    f" The infrastructure score is "
                    f"{infrastructure_score}/100."
                )

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # Infrastructure highlights
            # -------------------------------------------------

            categories = infrastructure.get(
                "categories",
                {}
            )

            infrastructure_highlights = []

            for category, data in categories.items():

                nearest = data.get(
                    "nearest"
                )

                if not nearest:
                    continue

                name = nearest.get(
                    "name"
                )

                distance = nearest.get(
                    "distance_km"
                )

                if name and distance is not None:
                    infrastructure_highlights.append(
                        (
                            f"{name} is "
                            f"{distance} km away"
                        )
                    )

            if infrastructure_highlights:

                message += (
                    " Nearby infrastructure includes "
                    + ", ".join(
                        infrastructure_highlights[:3]
                    )
                    + "."
                )

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # Comparable market analysis
            # -------------------------------------------------

            comparable_metrics = property_analysis.get(
                "comparables",
                {}
            ).get(
                "metrics",
                {}
            )

            comparable_count = comparable_metrics.get(
                "priced_comparables",
                0
            )

            average_price = comparable_metrics.get(
                "average_price"
            )

            median_price = comparable_metrics.get(
                "median_price"
            )

            average_price_per_area = comparable_metrics.get(
                "average_price_per_area"
            )

            if comparable_count:

                message += (
                    f" I found {comparable_count} priced "
                    "comparable properties."
                )

                if average_price is not None:
                    message += (
                        f" Their average price is "
                        f"${average_price:,.0f}"
                    )

                if median_price is not None:
                    message += (
                        f", with a median of "
                        f"${median_price:,.0f}"
                    )

                if average_price_per_area is not None:
                    message += (
                        f" and an average price per "
                        f"sqm of "
                        f"${average_price_per_area:,.2f}"
                    )

                message += "."

        return message

    return (
        f"I found {len(results)} properties "
        "matching your query."
    )

Step 4 — Update the function call

You currently have:

message = build_message(
    query=query,
    understood=understood,
    results=structured_results,
    semantic_results=semantic_results,
)

Change it to:

message = build_message(
    query=query,
    understood=understood,
    results=structured_results,
    semantic_results=semantic_results,
    analysis=analysis,
)

That's important because build_message() now needs access to the analysis.

Marriott Residences Oman is available at AIDA, Muscat, Oman and is developed by DarGlobal with Marriott branding. The infrastructure score is 71.55/100. Nearby infrastructure includes Muscat International Airport is 12.8 km away, Sultan Qaboos University Hospital is 24.53 km away, and Sultan Qaboos University is 24.74 km away. Your current output has:

"airport": {
  "distance_km": 12.8,
  "score": 100
}

That is because your model defines 15 km or less as ideal for an airport.

That's fine if intentional, but later we'll want to review these scoring rules rather than treating them as objectively correct.

        ↓
Investment analysis

"Compare Marriott Residences with Trump Maldives"
        ↓
Property-vs-property analysis

ChatGPT said:

Yes — this is now working end-to-end. The next step should be to improve the /api/chat response from a raw data dump into an actual real-estate intelligence response, while keeping the existing engines intact.
Next step: Add an AI intelligence layer

Right now /api/chat successfully does:

    Query understanding
    Hybrid property search
    Infrastructure analysis
    Comparable analysis
    Market metrics
    Returns everything as JSON

The missing piece is interpretation.

For example, instead of only returning:

    Infrastructure score: 71.55
    Average comparable price: $966,666.67
    Median comparable price: $950,000
    3 comparables

the API should eventually be able to answer:

    Marriott Residences Oman is a branded hotel-residence project in AIDA, Muscat, developed by DarGlobal with Marriott branding.

    Its infrastructure score is 71.55/100. Shopping and airport access are strong, while hospital and school proximity are weaker relative to the scoring model.

    Three comparable Muscat hotel residences were identified, with prices ranging from $850K to $1.1M. The median comparable price is $950K and the average is approximately $966.7K.

    Based on the available evidence, the project has moderate-to-strong infrastructure support, but pricing confidence is limited because the target property's own price and unit size are currently unavailable.

Recommended implementation order

Step 1 — Create an intelligence/report builder

Add:

app/ai/analysis_builder.py

Its job should take:

property
infrastructure
comparables
comparable_metrics

and produce structured conclusions such as:

{
  "summary": "...",
  "strengths": [],
  "risks": [],
  "market_position": "...",
  "pricing": {
    "available": false,
    "average_comparable_price": 966666.67,
    "median_comparable_price": 950000.0
  },
  "infrastructure": {
    "score": 71.55,
    "assessment": "moderate"
  },
  "confidence": 0.9
}

Step 2 — Integrate it into property_analysis.py

Your existing:

analyze_property()

is already the correct orchestration point.

Don't rewrite the infrastructure or comparable engines. Add the intelligence layer after those calculations.

Step 3 — Add the analysis to /api/chat

Your current chat() already has:

structured_results = retrieval["results"]
semantic_results = retrieval["semantic_results"]

For each structured property, call the analysis service and return something like:

{
  "message": "...",
  "intent": "property_search",
  "results": [...],
  "analysis": [...]
}

You're already returning analysis, so this is a natural place to improve the implementation.

Step 4 — Improve confidence

There is one important issue in the current output:

"confidence": 1.0

Infrastructure confidence is 1.0, but the target property has:

price = null
area = null
bedrooms = null

So we should distinguish:

    Infrastructure confidence
    Comparable confidence
    Data completeness
    Overall intelligence confidence

That will make the system much more credible.

Step 5 — Add investment signals

Once the above works, the next intelligence layer can calculate:

    Infrastructure strength
    Comparable pricing position
    Developer/brand strength
    Location attractiveness
    Data completeness
    Potential risks
    Investment attractiveness
    Overall score

Then the API becomes an actual Real Estate Intelligence API, rather than just a property-search API.
I recommend we do this next

Let's implement Step 1: app/ai/analysis_builder.py first.

It should be deterministic initially — no LLM required yet. That gives us reliable, testable intelligence from the data you already have. After that, we can optionally put the LLM on top to turn the structured intelligence into natural-language answers.

Your current backend is at a good checkpoint: search → infrastructure → comparables → combined analysis → chat are all functioning. Now we move from retrieval to intelligence.
