Great. Let's implement Step 1: the deterministic intelligence/report builder.

Create this file:

app/ai/analysis_builder.py

Use this code:
analysis_builder.py

from future import annotations

from typing import Any

def _infrastructure_assessment(
score: float | None,
) -> str:
if score is None:
return "unknown"

if score >= 80:
    return "strong"

if score >= 60:
    return "moderate"

if score >= 40:
    return "weak"

return "very weak"

def _build_infrastructure_summary(
infrastructure: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
score = infrastructure.get(
"infrastructure_score"
)

categories = infrastructure.get(
    "categories",
    {},
)

strengths = []
weaknesses = []

for category, data in categories.items():
    category_score = data.get("score")

    if category_score is None:
        continue

    if category_score >= 80:
        strengths.append(category)

    elif category_score < 50:
        weaknesses.append(category)

return {
    "score": score,
    "assessment": _infrastructure_assessment(
        score
    ),
    "strengths": strengths,
    "weaknesses": weaknesses,
}

def _build_pricing_analysis(
comparable_metrics: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
comparable_count = comparable_metrics.get(
"comparable_count",
0,
)

priced_comparables = comparable_metrics.get(
    "priced_comparables",
    0,
)

average_price = comparable_metrics.get(
    "average_price"
)

median_price = comparable_metrics.get(
    "median_price"
)

average_price_per_area = comparable_metrics.get(
    "average_price_per_area"
)

return {
    "available": priced_comparables > 0,
    "comparable_count": comparable_count,
    "priced_comparables": priced_comparables,
    "average_price": average_price,
    "median_price": median_price,
    "average_price_per_area": average_price_per_area,
}

def _calculate_data_completeness(
property_data: dict[str, Any],
) -> float:
fields = [
"title",
"project_name",
"developer",
"property_type",
"country",
"city",
"location",
"price",
"currency",
"bedrooms",
"bathrooms",
"area",
"area_unit",
"description",
"brands",
]

available = sum(
    1
    for field in fields
    if property_data.get(field) is not None
    and property_data.get(field) != ""
)

if not fields:
    return 0.0

return round(
    available / len(fields),
    2,
)

def _build_summary(
property_data: dict[str, Any],
infrastructure: dict[str, Any],
pricing: dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
name = (
property_data.get("project_name")
or property_data.get("title")
or "This property"
)

location_parts = [
    property_data.get("location"),
    property_data.get("city"),
    property_data.get("country"),
]

location = ", ".join(
    str(value)
    for value in location_parts
    if value
)

summary = name

if location:
    summary += f" is located in {location}"

developer = property_data.get("developer")

if developer:
    summary += f" and is developed by {developer}"

infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
    "score"
)

if infrastructure_score is not None:
    summary += (
        f". Its infrastructure score is "
        f"{infrastructure_score}/100"
    )

if pricing.get("available"):
    median_price = pricing.get(
        "median_price"
    )

    if median_price is not None:
        summary += (
            f". The median comparable price is "
            f"${median_price:,.2f}"
        )

return summary + "."

def build_property_intelligence(
property_data: dict[str, Any],
infrastructure_data: dict[str, Any],
comparable_data: dict[str, Any],
comparable_metrics: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
"""
Build deterministic real-estate intelligence
from property, infrastructure, and comparable data.
"""

infrastructure = _build_infrastructure_summary(
    infrastructure_data
)

pricing = _build_pricing_analysis(
    comparable_metrics
)

data_completeness = _calculate_data_completeness(
    property_data
)

strengths = list(
    infrastructure["strengths"]
)

risks = list(
    infrastructure["weaknesses"]
)

if not property_data.get("price"):
    risks.append(
        "Target property price is unavailable"
    )

if not property_data.get("area"):
    risks.append(
        "Target property area is unavailable"
    )

comparable_count = comparable_metrics.get(
    "comparable_count",
    0,
)

if comparable_count < 3:
    risks.append(
        "Limited comparable-property evidence"
    )

if infrastructure["assessment"] == "strong":
    strengths.append(
        "Strong infrastructure profile"
    )

elif infrastructure["assessment"] == "moderate":
    strengths.append(
        "Moderate infrastructure profile"
    )

if pricing["available"]:
    strengths.append(
        "Comparable market pricing is available"
    )

confidence_components = []

infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
    "score"
)

if infrastructure_score is not None:
    confidence_components.append(
        infrastructure_data.get(
            "confidence",
            0.0,
        )
    )

if comparable_count >= 5:
    confidence_components.append(1.0)

elif comparable_count >= 3:
    confidence_components.append(0.8)

elif comparable_count >= 1:
    confidence_components.append(0.5)

else:
    confidence_components.append(0.0)

confidence_components.append(
    data_completeness
)

confidence = (
    sum(confidence_components)
    / len(confidence_components)
    if confidence_components
    else 0.0
)

return {
    "summary": _build_summary(
        property_data,
        infrastructure,
        pricing,
    ),
    "strengths": strengths,
    "risks": risks,
    "infrastructure": infrastructure,
    "pricing": pricing,
    "data_completeness": data_completeness,
    "confidence": round(
        confidence,
        2,
    ),
}

Here is the complete indented file for app/ai/analysis_builder.py:

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any


def _infrastructure_assessment(
    score: float | None,
) -> str:
    if score is None:
        return "unknown"

    if score >= 80:
        return "strong"

    if score >= 60:
        return "moderate"

    if score >= 40:
        return "weak"

    return "very weak"


def _build_infrastructure_summary(
    infrastructure: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    score = infrastructure.get(
        "infrastructure_score"
    )

    categories = infrastructure.get(
        "categories",
        {},
    )

    strengths = []
    weaknesses = []

    for category, data in categories.items():
        category_score = data.get("score")

        if category_score is None:
            continue

        if category_score >= 80:
            strengths.append(category)

        elif category_score < 50:
            weaknesses.append(category)

    return {
        "score": score,
        "assessment": _infrastructure_assessment(
            score
        ),
        "strengths": strengths,
        "weaknesses": weaknesses,
    }


def _build_pricing_analysis(
    comparable_metrics: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    comparable_count = comparable_metrics.get(
        "comparable_count",
        0,
    )

    priced_comparables = comparable_metrics.get(
        "priced_comparables",
        0,
    )

    average_price = comparable_metrics.get(
        "average_price"
    )

    median_price = comparable_metrics.get(
        "median_price"
    )

    average_price_per_area = comparable_metrics.get(
        "average_price_per_area"
    )

    return {
        "available": priced_comparables > 0,
        "comparable_count": comparable_count,
        "priced_comparables": priced_comparables,
        "average_price": average_price,
        "median_price": median_price,
        "average_price_per_area": average_price_per_area,
    }


def _calculate_data_completeness(
    property_data: dict[str, Any],
) -> float:
    fields = [
        "title",
        "project_name",
        "developer",
        "property_type",
        "country",
        "city",
        "location",
        "price",
        "currency",
        "bedrooms",
        "bathrooms",
        "area",
        "area_unit",
        "description",
        "brands",
    ]

    available = sum(
        1
        for field in fields
        if property_data.get(field) is not None
        and property_data.get(field) != ""
    )

    if not fields:
        return 0.0

    return round(
        available / len(fields),
        2,
    )


def _build_summary(
    property_data: dict[str, Any],
    infrastructure: dict[str, Any],
    pricing: dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    name = (
        property_data.get("project_name")
        or property_data.get("title")
        or "This property"
    )

    location_parts = [
        property_data.get("location"),
        property_data.get("city"),
        property_data.get("country"),
    ]

    location = ", ".join(
        str(value)
        for value in location_parts
        if value
    )

    summary = name

    if location:
        summary += f" is located in {location}"

    developer = property_data.get("developer")

    if developer:
        summary += f" and is developed by {developer}"

    infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
        "score"
    )

    if infrastructure_score is not None:
        summary += (
            f". Its infrastructure score is "
            f"{infrastructure_score}/100"
        )

    if pricing.get("available"):
        median_price = pricing.get(
            "median_price"
        )

        if median_price is not None:
            summary += (
                f". The median comparable price is "
                f"${median_price:,.2f}"
            )

    return summary + "."


def build_property_intelligence(
    property_data: dict[str, Any],
    infrastructure_data: dict[str, Any],
    comparable_data: dict[str, Any],
    comparable_metrics: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Build deterministic real-estate intelligence
    from property, infrastructure, and comparable data.
    """

    infrastructure = _build_infrastructure_summary(
        infrastructure_data
    )

    pricing = _build_pricing_analysis(
        comparable_metrics
    )

    data_completeness = _calculate_data_completeness(
        property_data
    )

    strengths = list(
        infrastructure["strengths"]
    )

    risks = list(
        infrastructure["weaknesses"]
    )

    if not property_data.get("price"):
        risks.append(
            "Target property price is unavailable"
        )

    if not property_data.get("area"):
        risks.append(
            "Target property area is unavailable"
        )

    comparable_count = comparable_metrics.get(
        "comparable_count",
        0,
    )

    if comparable_count < 3:
        risks.append(
            "Limited comparable-property evidence"
        )

    if infrastructure["assessment"] == "strong":
        strengths.append(
            "Strong infrastructure profile"
        )

    elif infrastructure["assessment"] == "moderate":
        strengths.append(
            "Moderate infrastructure profile"
        )

    if pricing["available"]:
        strengths.append(
            "Comparable market pricing is available"
        )

    confidence_components = []

    infrastructure_score = infrastructure.get(
        "score"
    )

    if infrastructure_score is not None:
        confidence_components.append(
            infrastructure_data.get(
                "confidence",
                0.0,
            )
        )

    if comparable_count >= 5:
        confidence_components.append(1.0)

    elif comparable_count >= 3:
        confidence_components.append(0.8)

    elif comparable_count >= 1:
        confidence_components.append(0.5)

    else:
        confidence_components.append(0.0)

    confidence_components.append(
        data_completeness
    )

    confidence = (
        sum(confidence_components)
        / len(confidence_components)
        if confidence_components
        else 0.0
    )

    return {
        "summary": _build_summary(
            property_data,
            infrastructure,
            pricing,
        ),
        "strengths": strengths,
        "risks": risks,
        "infrastructure": infrastructure,
        "pricing": pricing,
        "data_completeness": data_completeness,
        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

One small note: comparable_data is intentionally accepted by build_property_intelligence() but not used yet. We'll use it in the next step when we add deeper comparable-property interpretation.

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.comparable_engine import (
    calculate_comparable_metrics,
    find_comparables,
)
from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
    analyze_property_infrastructure,
)

from app.ai.analysis_builder import (
    build_property_intelligence,
)


def analyze_property(
    db: Session,
    property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Generate a combined intelligence report for a property.

    Combines:
    - property information
    - location information
    - infrastructure analysis
    - comparable properties
    - comparable market metrics
    """

    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return {
            "property_found": False,
            "property_id": property_id,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Location
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    location = None

    if property_item.location_record:
        location = {
            "id": property_item.location_record.id,
            "name": property_item.location_record.name,
            "type": property_item.location_record.location_type,
            "city": property_item.location_record.city,
            "country": property_item.location_record.country,
            "area": property_item.location_record.area,
            "address": property_item.location_record.address,
            "latitude": property_item.location_record.latitude,
            "longitude": property_item.location_record.longitude,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Infrastructure
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
        db,
        property_id,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Comparable properties
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    comparable_result = find_comparables(
        db,
        property_id=property_id,
        limit=10,
    )

    comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
        comparable_result,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Property intelligence
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = build_property_intelligence(
        property_data={
            "id": property_item.id,
            "title": property_item.title,
            "project_name": property_item.project_name,
            "developer": property_item.developer,
            "property_type": property_item.property_type,
            "country": property_item.country,
            "city": property_item.city,
            "location": property_item.location,
            "price": property_item.price,
            "currency": property_item.currency,
            "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
            "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
            "area": property_item.area,
            "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
            "description": property_item.description,
            "brands": property_item.brands,
        },
        infrastructure_data=infrastructure,
        comparable_data=comparable_result,
        comparable_metrics=comparable_metrics
    )


    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Overall confidence
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    infrastructure_confidence = infrastructure.get(
        "confidence",
        0.0,
    )

    comparable_count = comparable_result.get(
        "comparable_count",
        0,
    )

    if comparable_count >= 5:
        comparable_confidence = 1.0
    elif comparable_count >= 3:
        comparable_confidence = 0.8
    elif comparable_count >= 1:
        comparable_confidence = 0.5
    else:
        comparable_confidence = 0.0

    confidence = (
        infrastructure_confidence * 0.5
        + comparable_confidence * 0.5
    )

    return {
        "property_found": True,

        "property": {
            "id": property_item.id,
            "title": property_item.title,
            "project_name": property_item.project_name,
            "developer": property_item.developer,
            "source": property_item.source,
            "property_type": property_item.property_type,
            "listing_type": property_item.listing_type,
            "country": property_item.country,
            "city": property_item.city,
            "location": property_item.location,
            "price": property_item.price,
            "currency": property_item.currency,
            "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
            "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
            "area": property_item.area,
            "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
            "status": property_item.status,
            "completion_date": property_item.completion_date,
            "brands": property_item.brands,
            "source_url": property_item.source_url,
        },

        "location": location,

        "infrastructure": infrastructure,

        "comparables": {
            "items": comparable_result.get(
                "comparables",
                [],
            ),
            "count": comparable_result.get(
                "comparable_count",
                0,
            ),
            "metrics": comparable_metrics,
        },
        
        "intelligence": intelligence,


        "confidence": round(
            confidence,
            2,
        ),
    }

Recommended app/services/property_analysis.py
app/services/property_analysis.py

from future import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models.property import Property
from app.services.comparable_engine import (
calculate_comparable_metrics,
find_comparables,
)
from app.services.infrastructure_engine import (
analyze_property_infrastructure,
)

from app.ai.analysis_builder import (
build_property_intelligence,
)

def analyze_property(
db: Session,
property_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
"""
Generate a combined intelligence report for a property.

Combines:
- property information
- location information
- infrastructure analysis
- comparable properties
- comparable market metrics
- property intelligence
"""

property_item = (
    db.query(Property)
    .filter(Property.id == property_id)
    .first()
)

if not property_item:
    return {
        "property_found": False,
        "property_id": property_id,
    }

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Location
# ---------------------------------------------------------

location = None

if property_item.location_record:
    location = {
        "id": property_item.location_record.id,
        "name": property_item.location_record.name,
        "type": property_item.location_record.location_type,
        "city": property_item.location_record.city,
        "country": property_item.location_record.country,
        "area": property_item.location_record.area,
        "address": property_item.location_record.address,
        "latitude": property_item.location_record.latitude,
        "longitude": property_item.location_record.longitude,
    }

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Infrastructure
# ---------------------------------------------------------

infrastructure = analyze_property_infrastructure(
    db,
    property_id,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Comparable properties
# ---------------------------------------------------------

comparable_result = find_comparables(
    db,
    property_id=property_id,
    limit=10,
)

comparable_metrics = calculate_comparable_metrics(
    comparable_result,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Property intelligence
# ---------------------------------------------------------

intelligence = build_property_intelligence(
    property_data={
        "id": property_item.id,
        "title": property_item.title,
        "project_name": property_item.project_name,
        "developer": property_item.developer,
        "property_type": property_item.property_type,
        "country": property_item.country,
        "city": property_item.city,
        "location": property_item.location,
        "price": property_item.price,
        "currency": property_item.currency,
        "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
        "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
        "area": property_item.area,
        "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
        "description": property_item.description,
        "brands": property_item.brands,
    },
    infrastructure_data=infrastructure,
    comparable_data=comparable_result,
    comparable_metrics=comparable_metrics,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Overall confidence
# ---------------------------------------------------------

confidence = intelligence.get(
    "confidence",
    0.0,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Final response
# ---------------------------------------------------------

return {
    "property_found": True,

    "property": {
        "id": property_item.id,
        "title": property_item.title,
        "project_name": property_item.project_name,
        "developer": property_item.developer,
        "source": property_item.source,
        "property_type": property_item.property_type,
        "listing_type": property_item.listing_type,
        "country": property_item.country,
        "city": property_item.city,
        "location": property_item.location,
        "price": property_item.price,
        "currency": property_item.currency,
        "bedrooms": property_item.bedrooms,
        "bathrooms": property_item.bathrooms,
        "area": property_item.area,
        "area_unit": property_item.area_unit,
        "status": property_item.status,
        "completion_date": property_item.completion_date,
        "brands": property_item.brands,
        "source_url": property_item.source_url,
    },

    "location": location,

    "infrastructure": infrastructure,

    "comparables": {
        "items": comparable_result.get(
            "comparables",
            [],
        ),
        "count": comparable_result.get(
            "comparable_count",
            0,
        ),
        "metrics": comparable_metrics,
    },

    "intelligence": intelligence,

    "confidence": round(
        confidence,
        2,
    ),
}

Then test it

Next step: move intelligence into /api/chat

Right now /api/chat is already returning "analysis", but its response is effectively exposing the raw infrastructure/comparable analysis.

The next architectural step should be:

User query
    ↓
/api/chat
    ↓
understand_query()
    ↓
hybrid_search()
    ↓
property IDs
    ↓
analyze_property()
    ↓
build_property_intelligence()
    ↓
final ChatResponse

That will let a query such as:

curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"message":"Is Marriott Residences Oman a good investment?"}'

eventually produce an answer based on:

    property facts
    infrastructure
    comparable pricing
    strengths
    risks
    data completeness
    confidence

rather than just:

    "Marriott Residences Oman is available at AIDA..."

I would make this the next implementation step before introducing the LLM. Once the deterministic intelligence layer is stable, the LLM can be added on top of it as a controlled explanation layer rather than being responsible for calculating the numbers.

API MODULE: /home/vishnu/real-estate-ai-poc/backend/app/api/properties.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db
from app.models.property import Property
from app.schemas.property import PropertyResponse

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/properties",
    tags=["properties"],
)


@router.get("", response_model=list[PropertyResponse])
def get_properties(
    source: str | None = None,
    city: str | None = None,
    listing_type: str | None = None,
    property_type: str | None = None,
    limit: int = Query(default=20, le=100),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = db.query(Property)

    if source:
        query = query.filter(Property.source == source)

    if city:
        query = query.filter(Property.city.ilike(f"%{city}%"))

    if listing_type:
        query = query.filter(
            Property.listing_type == listing_type
        )

    if property_type:
        query = query.filter(
            Property.property_type == property_type
        )

    return query.limit(limit).all()


@router.get("/{property_id}", response_model=PropertyResponse)
def get_property(
    property_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    property_item = (
        db.query(Property)
        .filter(Property.id == property_id)
        .first()
    )

    if not property_item:
        return {
            "error": "Property not found"
        }

    return property_item

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from app.db.database import get_db

)

"",
response_model=list[PropertyResponse],
)
def get_properties(
source: str | None = None,
city: str | None = None,
listing_type: str | None = None,
property_type: str | None = None,

default=20,

),

):

        Property.source == source

        Property.city.ilike(f"%{city}%")
    )

    query = query.filter(
        Property.listing_type == listing_type
    )

    query = query.filter(
        Property.property_type == property_type
    )

"/{property_id}",
response_model=PropertyResponse,

):

)
Important: route order

There's a subtle issue here.

You currently have:

@router.get("/{property_id}")

before:

@router.get("/{property_id}/analysis")

FastAPI/Starlette routing can make overlapping dynamic routes confusing. Put the /analysis route before /{property_id}.

So I recommend this final order:

@router.get("")
def get_properties(...):
    ...


@router.get("/{property_id}/analysis")
def get_property_analysis(...):
    ...


@router.get("/{property_id}")
def get_property(...):
    ...

Use this version:
app/api/properties.py — recommended route order

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import get_db
from app.models.property import Property
from app.schemas.property import PropertyResponse
from app.services.property_analysis import analyze_property

router = APIRouter(
prefix="/api/properties",
tags=["properties"],
)

@router.get(
"",
response_model=list[PropertyResponse],
)
def get_properties(
source: str | None = None,
city: str | None = None,
listing_type: str | None = None,
property_type: str | None = None,
limit: int = Query(
default=20,
le=100,
),
db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
query = db.query(Property)

if source:
    query = query.filter(
        Property.source == source
    )

if city:
    query = query.filter(
        Property.city.ilike(f"%{city}%")
    )

if listing_type:
    query = query.filter(
        Property.listing_type == listing_type
    )

if property_type:
    query = query.filter(
        Property.property_type == property_type
    )

return query.limit(limit).all()

@router.get(
"/{property_id}/analysis",
)
def get_property_analysis(
property_id: int,
db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
return analyze_property(
db=db,
property_id=property_id,
)

@router.get(
"/{property_id}",
response_model=PropertyResponse,
)
def get_property(
property_id: int,
db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
property_item = (
db.query(Property)
.filter(Property.id == property_id)
.first()
)

if not property_item:
    return {
        "error": "Property not found"
    }

return property_item

{

I recommend the next implementation sequence

    Keep property_analysis.py as the deterministic analysis layer.
    Update ChatResponse schema so analysis is explicitly supported.
    Update chat.py to call analyze_property() for the returned property IDs.
    Change build_message() to use the intelligence data.
    Test queries such as:
        "Marriott Residences Oman"
        "Is Marriott Residences Oman a good investment?"
        "What are the risks of Marriott Residences Oman?"
        "How does Marriott Residences Oman compare with other properties?"
    Only after that, connect the LLM so it explains the deterministic analysis rather than inventing the underlying numbers.

The key architectural principle now should be:

Database
   ↓
Deterministic engines
   ├── search
   ├── infrastructure
   ├── comparables
   └── intelligence
          ↓
       Evidence
          ↓
        LLM
          ↓
   Natural-language answer

That gives you a much more reliable real-estate AI system because the LLM will explain evidence rather than calculate or fabricate it.
